- **ork-check**: Conducts the CHECK phase: re-runs the verification against the baseline, goes through the five axes and consolidates a single verdict. Does not fix what it finds. - **ork-go**: Conducts the GO phase inside the thread's worktree: one atomic commit per task, baseline recorded before starting. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/core/assets/adapters-en/claude-code/agents/ork-go.md) · ★6)
